## Authorization and scope

The user requested more evaluation after the prompt changed, explicitly said usage is not an issue because resets are pending, and authorized a larger subset. The announced design is 60 previously unrun tasks from the remaining 83 DeepSWE1.1 tasks, two attempts per condition: **240 planned fresh slots**, no imported scores; network/provider-interrupted attempts receive additional fresh replacements under the October28 amendment. Conditions are bundled Codex0.163.0 instructions and the exact current custom prompt frozen as `prompts/override-coding.md`. GPT-6 Astra xhigh, medium verbosity, same Codex0.163.0/Pier0.3.1. No routine approval is needed for this batch.

The new prompt includes the October26 Coding rules. The requirement-first wording proposed in a later analysis was NOT applied and is NOT being tested. Do not alter the frozen prompts or add that proposal during this study.

Read `experiment-plan.json` before acting. Seed20261026; quotas20TypeScript/18Go/18Python/2JavaScript/2Rust, one task per repository.60distinct repositories,52not in the previous30-task study. All60tasks are new. Task order and balanced30ABBA/30BAAB allocation frozen before outcomes. Historical30-task results remain separate; do not pool them as if they tested this prompt.

## Current interruption policy (user amendment, October 28)

The user explicitly instructed: **"If any run is interrupted by either network or provider, rerun it."** Read `protocol-amendments/network-provider-reruns-20261028.json`. This supersedes the earlier choice to retain provider-interrupted zeros/passes as primary completed slots. For this current fresh60 study, archive every externally interrupted attempt with its original score and records, then run a fresh isolated replacement with the same frozen prompt/model/config. Select the first replacement not externally interrupted, irrespective of reward, including any valid model timeout. Do not retry ordinary model failures or valid semantic grader zeros. If the model completed and only grading was interrupted, recover grading on the same patch. Prior completed studies remain stopped.

240 is the planned-slot target, not a cap on infrastructure recovery executions. Interrupted originals are excluded from primary slot completion/efficiency and reported separately with their usage. Explain this post-start policy amendment and changed execution order in the final report. No best-of-N selection. No new paid preflights or additional reset-card authorization. Keep zero automatic native retries; each external failure is diagnosed from actual terminal provider/network evidence, archived, and resumed by the controller. After an active trial finishes, require no active worker, free shared flock and no dedicated containers before mutating queues or dispatching. If a new external interruption occurs, follow this policy without asking routine permission.

Archive under `infrastructure/attempts/<job>--external-interruption-aN-<date>/`. Move the entire original job (never delete), retain a provenance receipt and any old behavior/trace reviews under controller-review; keep original copies and rebase viewing references to archive paths. Then the original planned job directory is free for its replacement. Preserve all normally completed jobs. Local archives must mirror the remote move before pulling, or stale local native results would be mixed with a new attempt. Pull without --delete. `run_batch.inspect_completed` and `summarize.py` require native attestation.passed for normal completion and reject external nonzero agent exits even if an old interruption-review receipt exists. The October30 evidence-reviewed resource-limit exception accepts the documented Optique B2 identity-passed 8GiB model OOM with its original official zero; it is explicitly not normal completion and is not rerun. Original code/plan backups are under protocol-amendments/before-interruption-reruns-20261028-*.

## Before dispatch or continuation

Check actual mini process, exclusive lock, current job/events and containers. Also check associated host compose/buildx processes and dedicated BuildKit history after any setup timeout; a surviving build can outlive the worker and shared lock. Never duplicate active work. Consult `runner-process.json` for its actual PID. Do not confuse the initiating controller turn with the detached worker. A stopped worker is not permission to rerun completed model work.

The old Terminal-Bench, old override, completed12-slot, and completed120-slot queues must remain stopped. The original Ink operator-stop marker is retained and required by this runner. Do not remove it. Preserve Blender and wuitu-ota and all unrelated workloads. Never start an old launch.sh.

`run_batch.py` uses a shared exclusive flock, one trial at a time, zero automatic trial retries, and refuses unfinished existing job directories. Existing finished slots are reused on a justified worker continuation. Every valid0is retained. Cached task images/build cache may be pruned only after all four slots for that task, only on the dedicated socket and only with no active containers. Native trial containers are removed by Pier after logs/artifacts are collected.

Reuse prior calibration; do not run more oracle/nop/preflight trials. Only concrete infrastructure evidence justifies diagnostics or recovery. Preserve the original failed setup/model/grader records before any recovery. Never rerun a scored model or valid grader failure to seek a pass. For model-complete/grader-infrastructure failures, recover grading only. The new adapter has no old task-specific Polars/Koota/Task hooks because those tasks are excluded; if the same real runtime failure appears on a new task, diagnose from actual logs and apply a documented symmetric infrastructure repair, not a prompt change.

## Isolation and evidence

Each native agent gets a fresh container/home, memory use and generation disabled, no memory files, no bundled skills/apps/plugins/subagents, only the frozen conveying5.1.0. The pre-model app-server audit is a configuration check, not a paid preflight. The post-model native attestation verifies Codex0.163.0, exact frozen prompt, Astra xhigh and completion/official timeout. Preserve provider interruptions and report normal completion separately from official reward. Do not silently interpret an empty or missing log as a model failure.

Collect official reward and F2P/P2P counts for every slot. Use `summarize.py` after an awaited complete copy. Agent time excludes setup/grading/controller downtime; record all-attempt input/cache/output/reasoning tokens and outer tool calls. Review only visible messages, calls, outputs and submitted patches, not hidden reasoning. Retain per-job behavior evidence with source paths/line references. Do not publish raw transcripts, auth or proxy credentials.

Observe existing questions: source exploration before edits, actual entrypoints and independent expected results, new tests versus generated clients, repeated checks without new evidence, successful/failed check-driven repairs, requirement coverage, scope and truthful completion. Also review the added Coding rules: boundary revalidation, helper extraction, error handling, typing and native APIs. Do not infer reduced complexity or better reasoning merely from fewer lines or a self-report.

### Diagnosed timeout cleanup boundary, without changing the harness

Actual Pier start_environment calls outer asyncio.wait_for(environment.start). The compose helper awaits process.communicate and handles TimeoutError but not the propagated CancelledError, so the outer timeout leaves its subprocess alive; native setup retry can overlap it. This explains the **orphan**, not the underlying stalled registry transfer. Source excerpts and evidence in **infrastructure/optique-timeout-cleanup-diagnosis-20261030.json**. Minimal recovery above sufficed; no speculative harness refactor. A read-only ctr probe failed because its requested socket did not exist, so it supplies no download-state evidence. Docker session health warnings also occurred during successful Adaptix jobs; do not assert they caused this failure. The canceled error at end of history-log capture came from stopping that read-only log client, not a new model interruption.

No pre-fix failing check shown. Custom r2's generated client only prints maps and booleans; no assertions/failure exit. It labels flex:-1 invalid but output is an object, not a detected failure. Shared round trips can conceal the same error in both new methods; literal component expectations are stronger. Static unexercised custom r2 risk: its box/pair/radius branch distributes tokenizer tokens instead of matched longhand groups. With a compound fork such as margin-top = custom <number> and input custom 1 custom 2 it would split four tokens rather than the two matched components.
